  - **WP-F.**
    - **S1, the long-purge log line** (close point 5; `s3f.md` "Point 5"; the ruling: "ADR-057 carries it into docs/DECISIONS.md at the merge"). *Context:* the owner's re-open criterion for the known misses is how often they happen in the field, and nothing in the kit measured that. *Options* (`end-sooner-options.md` §4; `s3f.md:8216-8229`): S1 a log line; S2 a `MetricsSnapshot` field; S3 a `diagnosticReport()` key; S4 a signpost interval; N0 nothing; S2 to S4 each change the public API (NEEDS-HUMAN-REVIEW). *Decision:* S1, by ruling. `performPurge`'s first turn reads the injected clock right after `commitTurnMarksForTests?(false)`, with the job and group counts as the turn begins (O(1)), and again after `?(true)`; when the turn is strictly longer than `SyncEngine.turnTimeLimit` (0.010 s, C2's limit; `EngineCommands.swift:961-964`) it writes one `.info` line on `.sync`, `long purge turn: <ms> ms, <jobs> jobs, <groups> groups`, the milliseconds to two decimals (`logLongPurgeTurn`, `:1006-1013`). The line names no id, hash, URL, path or key; S1 adds no await and no spawn (so no REENTRANCY row), nothing on the main thread, uses the injected clock (C4) and changes no public API. *Consequences:* the text, category, level and threshold are a soft contract that adopting apps may parse: an app counts long purge turns through its own `LogSink` or `OSLogStore`; `OSLogSink` logs with privacy `.public` and maps `.info` to `OSLogType.info` (`AssetLog.swift:80`, `:145-146`), and whether the unified log keeps `.info` lines for an `OSLogStore` read is UNVERIFIED (C7; APPLE-API-VERIFICATION §5). The interval is the benches' first-turn interval (the seam marks): it excludes formatting the line and the join registration after it. Tests: two R-OBS-1 tests (`e7cf01a` red first, green from `f38dd8c`). Open survivor: `s1-late-start` (c23 #4). NEEDS-HUMAN-REVIEW only if S2 to S4 are chosen later. The ruling estimated 1.5-3 h.
    - **F-28's record, with the rulings' qualifications** (no separate F-28 record existed). Since Stage 3 close-out cycle 9 (S3-R11) every admission and space verdict is decided on the followed state: the follow barrier (ARCHITECTURE §7.10; design D3 "root" with the judges' amendments) replaces the case-by-case follows of cycles 3 to 8, whose class (a decision taken while a follow is owed differing from the one taken when the follow visits first) each verification cycle extended. The barrier guarantees the answers, MUSTs and invariants of a visit-first engine, not its order, place, timing or backoff (S3-R16; the residual risks RR-n); places are admitted on the live gates (S3-R17, S3-R18); a free-space read and freed bytes apply on arrival (S3-R11 R1). The RR family by ruling: RR-1 closed (its remainder is not a residual); RR-6 removed; RR-14 blocking and fixed; RR-18 withdrawn; RR-21 fixed except its residuals (PinEnd narrows ruling 3's residual); RR-23 sits within RR-16; RR-17 and RR-20 go to the owner and the orchestrator (Known risks); RR-24 and RR-25 are non-blocking differences (S3-R16 item 2); PROBE-C9-THROTTLE: the MUST wins, non-blocking, recorded as a residual risk with its probe (RR-7, `barrierAdmitsTheFollowsNewContentAsAtTheApply`; S3-R17 item 1); the other RRs are recorded in the draft for RESIDUAL-RISKS. The ENOSPC class is exempt by the owner's ruling (point 4).
    - **The fixes ratified** (`s3f.md` "Items"; ARCHITECTURE §7): S3F-1 (S3-CONC-04: a satisfied first conditions reading is an edge); S3F-4 (S3-PERF-01: shutdown in bounded turns, `shutdownStepsPerTurn` 512; amendment to ADR-039); S3F-5 (S3-PERF-02: the follow after an apply visits only the jobs the copy's change moved, bounded per turn); S3F-6 (S3-CONC-03: run progress order for a lagging subscriber); S3F-12 and S3F-13: S3-B-063 (`e575681`: lazy re-admission through `CircuitParkedOrder`), S3-B-064 (`e21472b`), S3-B-065 (`6bb4c1e`: a job only released groups wanted that a new interest joins is reinstated at its place in the queue its lane now selects, whatever lane the interest brings; `relane`'s back-of-queue re-queue stays for jobs that waited in their queue all along), S3-B-081 (`002844b`: a claim-only job's failed attempts count against its held lane's retry policy) and S3-B-082 (`3cf147d`: `invariantViolations()` flags a registered group in `releasing` only while its Release holds members) (amendments to ADR-053 and ADR-054); S3F-14 (S3-B-068…S3-B-070: the §12 regression names, `closingStore`); S3F-15 (S3-B-077, `937d793`: an alarm turn takes at most `dueDeadlinesPerTurn` (512) due deadlines, `DeadlineHeap.popDue(at:limit:)` finding the due prefix by binary search; S3-B-078's measurements); S3F-3, S3F-8 and S3F-9 (tests), S3F-10 (the uncovered Sync regions: tests where a seam reaches, the rest in §14), S3F-11 (M-N-09, -12, -14, -16, -20, -39: tests in `EngineBoundaryTests.swift`) and S3F-16 (comments, `50561da`). Recorded, no code: S3F-7(b) (S3-CONF-04: user-initiated work dispatched while the conditions are unknown, after the idle teardown, is not trimmed when a critical thermal reading then arrives; ADR-036's cap applies to dispatch decisions taken with the conditions known; amendment to ADR-036).

The Sync bundle had at `s3-base` 298 tests at 1 min, 2 at 3 and 1 at 4, and has at `e840b0e` 259 at 1, 381 at 3, 8 at 5 and 1 at 6. By display name 92 pre-existing tests changed guard (85 from 1 to 3 min, 4 from 1 to 5, 2 from 3 to 5, 1 from 4 to 6); of 362 new tests 296 have 3 min, 50 have 1 min, 2 have 5 min and 14 none; no test name was lost.

In this heading and on the owner review list as owner items: f3c's two open shapes with OS-0, which N32 took to the owner with the close (Known misses), and the owner's uncommitted `Package.swift`, which the orchestrator asks about before Stage 5's `--full`.
- **Known misses, known risks and the accepted FAIL** (recorded, not fixed; Stage 5's RESIDUAL-RISKS.md and VERIFICATION.md carry them).
  - **WP-A/WP-H.** `start racing stop` stays load-sensitive (about 0.25 s alone, 12.5-13.0 s in whole-target debug runs, 30-44.8 s under TSan at loads of about 3-21, over 64.8 s at load 168.9-223); nothing shows that three minutes suffices at ADR-055's loads of 441-834. The linear-time check is still a CPU-time ratio (about 31-34x against 64x, roughly 2x headroom; the original failure was about 66x). Owner information, not a tag: a published manifest that relies on an empty or `.` segment, a trailing slash or a backslash does not behave as with the prior art (D-6 refuses it on every fetch, and S3AH-1 quarantines it); no valid shipped fixture has one. The allocation tests read process-wide heap figures (macOS 27 and the iOS 27 simulator only).
  - **WP-G.** S3-B-094: a replaced root no commit has detected is used until a qualifying failure or a commit, a root check whose stat fails records nothing, and `withAsset` can lease from a root that vanished after its read (one `lstat` per qualifying failure, none per success). K-11's download before the candidate check (the hunter's probe 5 of 5 on `s3-base`, the confirmer 6 of 6; small impact, it needs an entry whose kind does not match the page). S3-B-093 is a known miss: P-9's sliced release survey at 100,000 entries is unmeasured. The model's (a′) and (a″) are limits of the model. `AssetClient.swift:142` stays schedule-dependent (0 in s3g's run and in the merged head's profile).
  - **WP-B.** P-13's per-unit wall time: one `unlink(2)` stalls about every 850-1,060 unlinks with the fan-out (490-550 flat), by count, whatever the store controls; under the owner's p99 no unit is spare (P1) and about 30 % of runs fail (P2), inside the accepted FAIL. The kill-then-power-loss crash test overran one minute in all 8 cases inside a full TSan Storage run at load 277 → 628 (62 follow-on `.cancelled` issues); S3-R4's condition is not met, so its guard stays one minute. S3B-13: user-initiated publications are quadratic (10k/5k 2.71-2.86; about 0.5-1 ms each at 10,000, inside C2; the bounded designs change `Snapshot/AssetSnapshot.swift`), deferred to Stage 5 with the owner's P-8 run. S3-B-011 at `maxEntries` (100,000, release, worst of runs): grace start 37.31 ms, the reaper queue's filter and sort 22.84 ms, `freeSpace()` 4.19 ms, the first present-table copy 3.33 ms, the first records copy 9.34 ms; WP-C's quota eviction of half 21.98 ms, `purge(.all)` 35.83 ms, `purge(.evictable)` 34.02 ms, orphan GC 18.43 ms; at 10,000 every hold is under 3.2 ms (S3-R5). OI-15 (one redundant download per evicted record whose content is live elsewhere), OI-12 (orphan GC's window can close on every run on a busy store), dooms WP-C counted not taken back (an R-CLN-4 under-eviction by one blob's bytes; not exercised), S3-B-015 (not reproduced), S3-B-016 (unoptimised builds copy the blob table on each gain), and `reapScaling` under load (S3-R5).

The worst of three for `purge(.evictable)` of one shared blob exceeded 10 ms (17.91525 ms in cycle 1, 11.047209 ms merged; S3-R5). `apply`'s hook still takes a blob of 10,000 holders in one turn (over 10 ms above about 6,000-6,500 holders; not re-measured; routed to WP-B). The dry run holds the actor 38.9 ms at 100,000 (OI-4 declined). Phase 3 can stay undecided under continuous access stamps (RE-S29, ruling R9). A blob can end up split (Decision S3C-2), with no new bytes on disk. An across-turns turn costs O(ids left), unmeasured for one blob held by `maxEntries` ids (its first turn could exceed 10 ms; S3-R5). Mutants C, F and S are masked in WP-C's oracle and flows, killed only by WP-B's targeted tests (the confirmer's runs). About 400 direct `.task.value`/`.result` joins in `Tests/` fall outside S3C-5.

R-NET-3's run-time half stays undetected (L5-R5). The oldest-first argument inherits `unclaimedFinishes`: a buffered earlier finish satisfies the next wait without a new wake (L5-R5, "a race WP-D tolerates"). G3 stays intact, and the six red cases become the to-do list for a re-open. Every later --full stays red until they're fixed." `verify.sh --full` on the final head is expected to end `VERIFY: FAIL (1 failed: benchmarks)`, WP-F's accepted end state only when its causes are the six purge shapes of "R-CLN-6: a purge's first turn over 10,000 ids holds the engine's actor at most C2's 10 ms" (`BenchSyncCycle22Purge.swift:15`, asserting at `:32`) and P-13 (WP-B's; S3-R26 item 10), each named from the benchmarks log, and every other stage passes on its own. G3 holds: no `withKnownIssue`, no `.disabled`, no change to `allCases`, `c2Budget` or the Benchmarks tree. A case red only at a 1-minute load above 10 follows rulings :763 with its loads (a miss is a best of 3 above `c2Budget` at a load of 10 or less; a figure seen only above 10 follows S3-R23 items 2 and 3). Any other failure is a new cause and goes to the owner. Commit messages name every red case (the six, P-13 and, on `e7cf01a`, S1's test); `commitTwoTokensTurns` (9.02 ms, reqs #11) is the case nearest the limit. "The /goal's Done is not met (`PROMPT.md:96`: `VERIFY: PASS` with passing benchmarks). The owner set it aside for WP-F with this answer, and Stage 5's report says so."
      - *Figures.* The close on `450022b`: 7 issues, the six plus P-13 (13.583 ms longest unit in 490 units, against `wp/s3f`'s maximum assertion); the verification on `d88bdb1`: exactly 7 (P-13 at 9.87 ms in 507 units). The P-13 cause at both was `wp/s3f`'s maximum assertion, which the merge replaced with the nearest-rank p99 (`d97e4e7`; ADR-050 option A). At the merged head (`.build-orch/merge-full.log`; benchmarks log `.verify-logs/benchmarks.log` of `.worktrees/merge-check`): 88 tests, 7 issues, the six plus `userToBound` (red only above a load of 10, a load-only figure under :763, not a new cause); P-13 passed there (p99 4.80 ms against 5.0, maximum 8.09 ms, 525 units), so the six were the only accepted causes present. Every other stage passed on `e840b0e`: gates-selftest, harness, toolchain; build-debug, build-release, build-ios; three test runs, each Transport 165, Sync 713, Storage 500, Model 89, Kit 90, Compatibility 71 and AppleHosted 104; coverage Sync 95.07 %, Storage 97.54 %, overall 96.92 %; TSan; trace (90 requirements, 78 MUST, 1661 tests with ids); unsafe; docs 1281/1281.
      - *P-13, inside the FAIL* (P1, P2, P7; `wf_19408ffa-9ba`). The nearest-rank p99 tolerates n − ceil(0.99 n) units over 5 ms (4 for n < 500, 5 for 500-599, 6 for n ≥ 600), and every run on `e840b0e` had exactly 5 over (525, 528, 509 and 519 units): no spare units. The p99 sample is itself a stalled unit, at 4.31-4.89 ms; p95 is about 0.99 ms and the unit's on-CPU maximum about 1.0 ms; the quiet-load p99 margin is 2-14 %, and the maxima (10.51, 9.70, 8.00 ms) would fail the old maximum assertion. About 30 % of runs on this unit fail the p99 with no code change (measurement lens: 17 of 53 recorded runs, Wilson 95 % 0.21-0.45, and a Poisson fit at a mean of 4.56 gives P(≥6) = 0.31; refuter: 14 of 50, none of the 9 runs since s3b's reaper-queue change would fail, but 5 of those 9 sit at the limit), some at a quiet load. A P-13 red with no code change falls inside the accepted FAIL, not a new cause. P-13 prints no load averages, unlike the C2 cases (S3-R23 item 1; recorded, not fixed). For the owner, information only, not a re-judgment (P8): P-13 now passes or fails on how many times APFS stalls an unlink in a run, not on the store's 1 ms unit bound; S3B-12 rated option A marginal and called a p98 robust; the owner ruled p99; only the owner can change the statistic or the number, and the bar stays as ruled.
    - **Point 4, the ENOSPC class: a known residual correctness risk, not blocking.** The owner chose "Yes, the whole class", described as: "A different answer between the two visit orders, caused by an event inside the disk-full window, is recorded, not fixed. Broken MUSTs, hangs and flaky tests there still block."; and earlier, N28 (S3-R28 item 1c): "Agreed let's document it as known risk". It replaces item 1c amended point 1's lead ("Only correct #1 and #2 are exempt") and its bullets "What is exempt", "Outside the exemption" and "Every other answer difference"; the wider readings of N29, N31 and ORCHESTRATION's Stage 3 section are superseded.
      - *Exempt:* an answer that differs between the two visit orders, caused by an event inside an ENOSPC window (S3-R16 1(a)); the window runs from a transfer's or a publication's ENOSPC end to the return of `spaceFailed`'s free-space read (item 1c amended, point 6(b)). N29's T20 test finds its members: "an answer that differs only because T20 (`EngineJobs.swift:360-364`) re-queues a job failed at an ENOSPC end in one order and not the other, before that job's space verdict, given at once or at the follow's end after spaceFailed's deferral (`EngineFailures.swift:573-582`)". Where the test and the owner's words differ, the owner's words decide; a late-drain difference is exempt only when its cause falls inside the window, otherwise it follows point 8 and the orchestrator asks the owner in one line whether it joins the class (none is confirmed today: W-JOIN-HOP in the late drain is UNCONFIRMED).
      - *Members:* measured, correct #1 (PROBE-C22C-TS-DRAIN-JOIN, seed 2_029_101: visited first A, X and V get `failed(insufficientSpace(required: 100000, available: 31318659))` with one start, swept first `ok(100000 B)` with two starts; the control, seed 2_029_201, answers alike) and correct #2 (PROBE-C22C-APPLY-IN-READ, seed 2_029_301: visited first available 31318658 with one start, swept first ok with two starts), each 2/2 on `9aa337c` and again on `d88bdb1` (c23 #8); suspected by code reading only (UNCONFIRMED; each a member only if a run shows the difference): W-AWAY, W-DB, W-JOIN-HOP at a publication's end, W-JOIN-HOP in the late drain, item 1's former step-0 shapes and `f1c-review.md` §6's shapes; a shape a verifier newly finds is included. Such an answer is recorded under "Known residual correctness risks (owner ruling 2026-11-06)" and is not reported as blocking.
      - *Still blocking inside the window* (each to the owner before any merge): a broken MUST or invariant, a hang or a job that nothing advances, and a flaky test (S3-R16 1(b) to 1(d)); 1(e) and 1(f), and every finding after the window, follow point 8. The user-visible effect (N29): the request was made before the end and replayed in the read; the probes cap retries at 1 (`maxAttemptsPerRun = 1`, `VerifyC22CorrectProbes.swift:258`, `:387`; the default user policy has 2) and script the second attempt to succeed. Why it is not fixed: four analyses were unsound (f1, f1b, f1c route A, f1c route B); route Q (a 3-5 h independent check, then 44-67 h; `f1c-review.md` §3, §9) is not taken, and Q itself leaves open W-OBO, W-PEND-LATE's tail, the holder-state approximation (W-BACK-PUB, W-NEST), W-SUPER, `lastError`'s figures and the out-of-class backlog (W-HOP-DEFERRED, W-PROMO, W-PEND-LATE's non-ENOSPC twin). No code, test or early probe is added (item 1c's freeze). Re-open criterion: field frequency.
    - **N33's open items (point 8; recorded, not fixed).** *c23 #4:* `s1-late-start` survives S1's test, because the ruling's test design moves the clock only at the `?(true)` mark, so the place of the turn's first clock read is right by review only; S1's code matches point 5 and is not a defect, so it is not reverted (re-open route: a seam mark inside the walk; `s1-groups-after` is equivalent, since the purge's first turn removes no group). *c23 #7,* a test-harness hang, not an engine hang: `EngineIntrospection.isIdleSilent` ignores an `applyAndFollow` running in the caller's own task, so a test or probe that applies from its own task and drives on `isIdleSilent` alone can leave an apply pending without noticing (PROBE-C23C-WALK2: 11 of 24 seeds hung past 900 s; stalled seeds 102, 112 and 116 had `applyTurnHeld` true, `applyQueue` 29-52 and a store sleeper 0.25 s ahead; one 1 s clock advance ended every apply, and all end checks then passed in 8/8 seeds); ARCHITECTURE §7.8's Idle adds the environment census (`ManualTimeSource.pendingSleepers == 0`), which would have caught them, while the harness's `settle()` checks `isIdleSilent` alone (re-open route: drive until `isIdleSilent && appliesInFlight == 0`; `appliesInFlight` is engine state, not in `EngineIntrospection`). Recorded as they are: c23 #8 (correct #1 and #2 still reproduce) and #9 (the accepted FAIL's figures).
    - **Other known risks** (non-blocking). *RR-24:* a shutdown inside the barrier answers `shutDown` to every answer that waits for the drain: visited first, a T20 retry started at a follow's end can serve its joins before a shutdown (probe f2-R1, serve forced first: ok(20 B) ×11, 2/2 on `9aa337c`, not committed); swept first, with the follow still owed, the same joins wait behind the verdict and get `shutDown` ×11 (3/3 in PROBE-C22C-SHUTDOWN-HELD-RETRY; A-T-SD pins it since `03c4e59`); it qualifies `EngineFollowBarrierTests.swift:1315` ("Shutdown has no visit-first counterpart"). *RR-25:* a purge swept first leaves a failed user job its backoff, where visited first the purge re-admits it (`withdrawPurgedJoins` takes the groups' joins before they reach the job; not probed: T-OS1 and T-OS1w were not built). *RR-17, for the owner:* an admission that races an older apply omitting the id answers `notFound` although the store lists the id (PROBE-C17-ADMIT-DURING-APPLY, seed 1_618_202, failed 2/2, not committed; pre-existing, both rules predate `s3-base`; outside the barrier class; visit-first gives the same answer); options (a) defer the decision to the catch-up's follow, (b) record the ids joined unlisted, neither taken. *RR-20, for the orchestrator and Stage 5:* when the owed move takes a job's only id, the publication binds nothing yet books a commit and an object success, its staged `.part` stays until staging GC (R-CLN-3), and the free-space estimate drops by its bytes until the next read; both orders answer alike (W gets `integrityFailure`; VERIFY-C18R-EMPTY-BINDING 2/2); the proposed fix (discard the staged file, retire the job without a commit) changes publication bookkeeping and is left open. *The barrier's admission wait* of a multi-turn follow is accepted (S3-R11 item 4, R2): 131-143 ms at 10,000 members to the follow's end (the ruling's figure, from D3 and the judges; the draft measured no admission for 141-149 ms after a release over 10,000 changed members with 10,000 waiting places). *Live-gate answer differences* (S3-R17 items 1-2), *WP-A's manifest parser under TSan* (S3-R10) and *the TSan guard margins* (S3-R12) are recorded in the draft. *The default-tuning case* of item 5's `followedLane` difference is UNCONFIRMED (with `shortInteractiveRetry`, a 30 s cap, a Retry-After of 60 s flips an answer; the default `maxRetryAfter` is 3,600 s; its probe was not run). *The strict R-INT-5 path:* under `allowUnverifiedAssets = false` a resolve of a checksum-less entry downloads the bytes before the store refuses them at publication (`integrityFailure`, not retried; observed behaviour, §14); an engine-side refusal before the download is the orchestrator's decision (Assignments). *The margin list and `userToBound`* (Benchmarks) are a known edge: a red at a load of 10 or less in a later `--perf` or in Stage 5's `--full` is a new cause and goes to the owner.

    - *other-wp:b:* a unit test of an apply that adds an id over a detached record (only WP-B can produce the record kinds that stay detached; WP-B owns the file); `AssetStore.recordFailure` commits the whole index once per call, so a burst of N 404s rewrites it N times (about 5 ms each at 10,000 records in release); P-13's records (the amendment to ADR-050) and its post-merge p99 margin of 2-14 %. *other-wp:b and other-wp:h:* Storage time-outs at loads of 100-540 (`StoreCrashTests.swift:167`, `LoopbackHTTPServerTests.swift:400`).
    - *other-wp:g:* Kit's follower and group-registration races; WP-G owns the files. `withAsset`'s "root vanished after every reopen", routed here too, is closed: S3G-3 tests it (`FacadeReopenLimitTests`), and §14 records `AssetClient.swift:148-149` and `:151` as run since Stage 3.
    - *The orchestrator (decisions, not WP-F's):* the strict R-INT-5 engine path (an engine-side refusal before the download would change what a user resolve answers and when; "R-INT-5: with unverified assets disallowed a resolve of a checksum-less entry fails integrityFailure at publication and is not retried", `EnginePublicationRefusalTests.swift`, pins today's behaviour); RR-20's proposed fix, with Stage 5; the one-line owner question for a late-drain difference caused outside the ENOSPC window (point 4); the post-merge list (close point 10): the `s3-merged` tag (never pushed unless the owner asks), removing the s3 worktrees and branches only once pushed or archived (`.build-orchestrator/` is never removed), the final archive snapshot with a restore test (cycle 23's evidence in `.worktrees/s3f/.build-s3f/c23/`, `.build-orchestrator/s3f-probes-c23`, and `.build-orch/`'s `merge-full.log`, `merge-userToBound-rerun1.log`, `merge-P13-quiet.log`, `t64b/`, `sim-merged/` and `mut-recheck/`), and `wp/s3f`'s tip (`c9e4d58`; origin's copy is stale at `9aa337c`) pushed or archived before the branch is removed (N25); pushes of `archive/build-orchestrator` and `feature/asset-kit` under the owner's standing permission (N31), never `main`, never force.
    - *`docs/REOPEN-WP-F.md`'s final version* (the orchestrator's; close point 10). v1 (`d88f025`) was written at `9aa337c`, before the close. The final version carries the close state (option (e), merged as `e840b0e`, the accepted FAIL); where `wp/s3f` lives; the base a re-open uses (the merged `feature/asset-kit`, whose Sync sources equal `c9e4d58`'s, with other stages' changes such as the moved `seconds(since:)`); the final snapshot with the c23 files; every known miss and residual (N7's five, the §7 variants and route A's third shape, APM, item 4's shapes, 12.1's shapes outside the bar, the open survivors and the three unreached lines, `releaseRerouteHolds` and the default-tuning probe, c23 #4 and #7 (a fix for #7 may need an `EngineIntrospection` contract addition, `appliesInFlight`), and P-13); N25's cases at f3c's reviewed figures (12.9-23.3 ms and about 21-34 ms, not 9-17 and 10-20); f3c and its review, and the signal the owner chose (S1); pointers to this ADR, VERIFICATION and RESIDUAL-RISKS; and the 16:30 list (N26-N30, item 1c's two risks, the owner's C2 ruling). Its stale facts (§1 "written at `9aa337c`"; `:30` "Cycle 23 is to build both routes"; `:39`, `:43`; `:47` f3c "not written"; `:50` "none at version 1"; the persistence wording at `:71-72`) are replaced. For a re-open: route C 19-28 h then item 3b 17-26.5 h (36-54.5 h); A-2 2.5-4 h more; f3c's core 12-18.5 h on top; route Q a 3-5 h check, then 44-67 h; APM and item 4 not estimated; S1's alternatives S2-S4 are public API (NEEDS-HUMAN-REVIEW); S1's kill is a seam mark inside the walk, and c23 #7's harness drives until `isIdleSilent && appliesInFlight == 0`; the scratch cases are in `.build-s3f/c23/bench0`, the latest arguments file is `s3-fix-args-run13.json`.
    - *Stage 4 (adoption):* S1's field signal works only if the app collects the kit's `.sync` `.info` lines, through its own `LogSink` (the `AssetEnvironment` log route) or by reading them with `OSLogStore`, whose retention is UNVERIFIED; without that, the owner's re-open criterion has no data. Stage 4 decides whether the carousel app collects them; any API that exposes a count (S2-S4) is a public change that needs human review.
    - *Stage 5:* its `--full` FAIL at benchmarks is the accepted end state only when the six (and P-13) are its causes; a P-13 red is inside the FAIL (about 30 % of runs fail with no code change); a red at a 1-minute load of 10 or less in a margin-list case (`commitTwoTokensTurns`, `userSharedApplyTurns(.keep)`, `divergenceTurns`, `concentrationCommitRerouteTurns`) or in `userToBound` is a new cause and goes to the owner; a red seen only above 10 follows rulings :763 (re-run alone at a quiet load, loads recorded). The report says that the owner set the /goal's Done aside, and (a reader's note, not a ruling) still shows the /goal's other conditions: GATES-CONFIG sha256, HARNESS unchanged, the COVERAGE floors, TRACE, UNSAFE and DOCS PASS, 3 consecutive passing test runs and a passing TSan step; Stage 4's ZTronCarouselCore build with its CDN tests passing through the shims; VERIFICATION.md listing everything not verified. Nobody touches the owner's `Package.swift`; the orchestrator asks the owner about it before that `--full`. Stage 5 writes `VERIFICATION.md` (this ADR's WP-F "Not verified" items, with what point 8's verification did not reach), `DEVICE-TEST-PLAN.md` (C2 figures on a device; `OSLogStore`'s retention of S1's line; S3-B-066's churn), `RESIDUAL-RISKS.md` (RR-24 and RR-25, and the other RRs the draft records for it, RR-7 among them; the ENOSPC class under "Known residual correctness risks (owner ruling 2026-11-06)"; the known misses; f3c's open shapes marked OPEN; c23 #4 and #7; P-13's P1 and P2; the margin list; RR-17 marked for the owner) and `MUTATION-PROBES.md` (new; SPEC §8 and §7.4: discovery's 64 records, `.build-orchestrator/s3-hunt-result.json` "mutate", the 11 survivors' re-check results and point 6's mutant runs), and regenerates TRACEABILITY.md. The TSan Sync bundle runs alone, or paired only at an S3-R12 rule-1 mean of 30 or less (the 1-minute load sampled every 5 s or less, from 60 s after the later bundle's start; at loads 50-78 the paired bundles timed out 44 and 38 one-minute tests, on time limits only).
    - *Test authors:* a test or probe that applies from its own task must not drive on `isIdleSilent` alone (c23 #7); the model tests' liveness phase (S3-B-079) remains open; every later `--full` stays red at benchmarks until the six purge cases are fixed (close point 3).

  - WP-G: none; §10.6 is restated unchanged with the code's citation counts.
  - WP-B: no new row; R13 restated for the counted releases and their take-back, and R7 for the cursor's carried keys (§10.1).

  - WP-D: D-9 amended (§10.4: the newest-request serial and the rewrite loop; "nothing is read after the last await" removed) and RE-S42 restated (§10.2: one completion per handler, oldest first); the control task's three awaits run in a task owned by a class, not in actor code.

RE-S21 (the answer follow-ups), RE-S22 (`closeStore()`), RE-S24 (the purge's first turn), RE-S37 (the lifecycle before the view is followed) and f-4 (the follow barrier) are widened. The code cites the new rows as "(proposed)" in 11 `//` comments (`EngineShutdown.swift:180`, `:193`, `:212`; `EngineLifecycle.swift:496`, `:504`; `EngineDispatch.swift:856`, `:872`, `:1044`; `EngineFollow.swift:435`, `:1624`, `:1680`), as the earlier rows' citations do (the word is historical). S1 adds no await and no row.

- **Process.**
  - **Record label.** The drafts write "Orchestrator ruling (close-out)"; the rulings files' term decides, and this ADR cites them as "Orchestrator ruling (Stage 3)".
  - **Contract `///` beyond S3-R6's bodies-only** are ratified here, not reverted: `TransferID.swift:124-125` and `ManualTimeSource.swift:144-147` (WP-A/WP-H), and the WP-B, WP-C and WP-D ones listed under "Ratified contract changes".
  - **TRACEABILITY** was regenerated on the merged head (`8d86acb`): R-MAN-2 22→25, R-SEC-2 10→12, R-ENV-1 12→14, P-8 2→3, R-CAR-3 12→13, R-STO-9 28→31, R-SYN-12 35→51.
  - **Merge order.** S3-R7 orders the merges ah, b, c, d, f, g; WP-G merged on 30 Oct (`d59bcc4`), before WP-F (`e840b0e`, 6 Nov). S3-R7 also says no close-out depends on another's Stage 3 change, and the merged head's runs pass with it; the deviation is recorded, not undone.
  - **S3-B-095's listing.** S3G-5 copied `subscribe()`'s registration from `g.md:183`; ADR-054's "Rulings over the draft" ruled that listing stale, so this ADR omits it.
  - **`reapScaling`'s guard.** The benchmark has carried `.timeLimit(.minutes(3))` since `8b3fc89` with no measured duration (its suite ran 31.091 s in release). S3-R4 is about hang guards under TSan; a release-only benchmark is outside it, and benchmarks elsewhere carry guards of 3 to 20 minutes. Recorded, not re-measured.
  - **"Test support:" display names.** S3-R2 asks for a requirement-ID prefix; WP-C's `PollReleaseTests` uses "Test support:", as WP-H's `CancelledWaitTests` did, since no requirement ID names a harness. The precedent is ratified here for harness tests (the trace gate passed on the merged head).
  - **S3-R3 deviation (WP-D cycle 1).** The M-N-22 and S2-19 control-reader mutants were applied in the s3d worktree itself and removed with `git checkout`, not in a scratch copy; mitigation: the cycle-2 requirements verifier re-applied both in its own scratch copy and both failed (`.build-orchestrator/s3d-probes-c2/retry-reqs-mutants.out:37-42`, 3 and 1 issues out of 161 tests), and every cycle-3 mutant ran in `.build-s3d/c3/mutpkg`.
  - **WP-F's records** (close point 6). *The 21 findings of cycle 22 at the final head* (`s3f.md:8027-8052`): correct #1 and #2 a known risk (point 4; route Q not taken); correct #3 closed by `03c4e59` and confirmed by the verification; correct #4 and reqs #1 a known miss and a cause of the accepted FAIL (route C plus item 3b not taken); correct #5 and reqs #2: APM's turn a known miss, item 4's shapes unproven (routes not estimated); correct #6 and reqs #4 open: the three unreached lines `EngineCommands.swift:1025`, `:1028` and `:1033` (`:1001`, `:1004`, `:1009` at `9aa337c`); correct #7 and reqs #3 closed for run 12's five kill tests and `p2-after-verdict-replay-rule` (mut-k23, 2/2 in each order that reaches the branch, by assertions) and for e1-P1's other side (`64f276d`), `p2-after-verdict-true` killed 2/2 swept first by the verifier (c23 #5, not independently verified), and open: r2b, b22-no-run-owner, e1-R1's kill table, `i5-pin-fold` (T-E2), `ps-write-always`'s swept-first side and the default-tuning probe; correct #8 P-13, WP-B's; correct #9 unproven (with point 2's entry 5); correct #10 no action; reqs #5 closed by `3a551f4`, `79e1e0b`, `6c830b8`, `5672c6d`, `ab82a5a` and `450022b`; reqs #6 and #9 by `79e1e0b`; `486ee2a` kills p7 (`EngineFollow.swift:762`) and r5 (`EngineFailures.swift:841`); `8b252f2` kills r8 (`:838`); `06880a8` kills vy (`EngineMoves.swift:308`); `9e9a500` kills r2c (`EngineFollow.swift:704`);   - **C7 evidence for the standard library.** The draft cites the stdlib signatures `EngineState.dropMember(_:waitingFor:)` uses, verified in the macOS 27.0 and iOS 27.0 SDKs' `Swift.swiftinterface` at the same lines (`Dictionary.index(forKey:)` 7807, `Values.subscript(position:)`'s getter 8050-8053, `Dictionary.remove(at:)` 7908-7909, `Set.remove` 21147-21149, `_Variant.remove` 22111-22123, `_NativeSet.find` 16351-16353, `Dictionary._Variant.removeValue(forKey:)` 9125 and `find` 9137); they stay here and in the draft, not in APPLE-API-VERIFICATION, because no new Apple framework API entered Sync in Stage 3 (`git diff s3-base` adds only `import Foundation` and `import ZTronAssetModel` under `Sources/`).
  - **Test file lists.** The merge added 97 test files under `Tests/ZTronAssetSyncTests` and modified 37 more; ARCHITECTURE §1.2 takes the list from git (`git diff --name-status --diff-filter=A e840b0e^1 e840b0e -- Tests/ZTronAssetSyncTests`), not from the draft's per-cycle prose.

They are recorded as this pass's, not independently verified.
